- **cosightbot-material**: 资料目录绑定与知识库构建：绑定本地资料目录、把文件/目录加入知识库（默认会话级， --global 进 agent 级）、删除已入库文件、查看索引状态、手动刷新索引。 在用户要求"绑定资料目录/添加文件到知识库/删除知识库文件/资料有更新同步一下"时触发。 输入为目录或文件路径，输出为操作结果 JSON；所有资料操作完成后自动执行增量构建。 ([ZTE-AICloud/Co-Sight](https://github.com/ZTE-AICloud/Co-Sight/tree/HEAD/Co-Sight%20Harness/cosight-bot/skills/cosightbot-material) · ★1.1k)
- **cosightbot-retrieve**: 个人知识库检索问答：拿章节树（merged_overview）、按 [F###] 节点读文档全文、 pre-processed 层全文扫描。纯只读——绝不触发索引构建。 在任何业务/资料类问题时默认触发（不依赖用户明说"查资料"）："XX 怎么配置的 / 上周提了哪些问题 / 这个方案的进展 / 资料里有没有 XX"。 输入为用户问题，输出为基于实读文档的答案（附文档来源）。 ([ZTE-AICloud/Co-Sight](https://github.com/ZTE-AICloud/Co-Sight/tree/HEAD/Co-Sight%20Harness/cosight-bot/skills/cosightbot-retrieve) · ★1.1k)
- **cosightbot-soc-analyze**: Excel 标书通读与方案提炼：把标书 Excel 转 Markdown 后按 Sheet 切分， Map-Reduce 并发调用大模型，输出 8 段结构化分析报告（标书概况/客户需求/ 硬件/软件/外购/厂商锁定/技术架构/综合结论）。在用户提供标书 Excel 并希望 "快速通读/分析这份标书/提炼客户需求与方案"时触发。仅打印报告，不修改 Excel。 ([ZTE-AICloud/Co-Sight](https://github.com/ZTE-AICloud/Co-Sight/tree/HEAD/Co-Sight%20Harness/cosight-bot/skills/cosightbot-soc-analyze) · ★1.1k)
- **cosightbot-soc-answer**: 单条标书需求的深度应答：同时检索产品规范与历史范式，调用大模型做语义融合、 合规判定（FC/PC/NC）与多文档冲突检测，产出带全量引文溯源的 Markdown 应答。 在用户说"应答/答复/解答 某条需求"、或直接抛出一个标书条款问题时触发（默认路径）； 仅当用户明确说"搜索/检索/查一下"时才走普通检索而非本技能。 ([ZTE-AICloud/Co-Sight](https://github.com/ZTE-AICloud/Co-Sight/tree/HEAD/Co-Sight%20Harness/cosight-bot/skills/cosightbot-soc-answer) · ★1.1k)
- **cosightbot-soc-extract**: 从 Excel 标书中提取待应答需求条目：甄别需求 sheet、剥离填表指南等非需求页， 用 Python（openpyxl）把需求编号/描述/合规列/备注/参考文件抽取为结构化 JSON， 产物 `&lt;stem&gt;-需求提取.json` 经 validate.py 硬性自检后进入应答流水线。 在用户提供标书 Excel 并要求"提取需求/开始应答流程"时触发。零兜底： 解析不了就报错，严禁编造条目。 ([ZTE-AICloud/Co-Sight](https://github.com/ZTE-AICloud/Co-Sight/tree/HEAD/Co-Sight%20Harness/cosight-bot/skills/cosightbot-soc-extract) · ★1.1k)
- **cosightbot-soc-fill**: 把应答 JSON 回填到标书 Excel：复制原标书另存 `&lt;stem&gt;-应答后.xlsx`（严禁覆盖原件）， 用 Python（openpyxl）按需求文本匹配把 FC/PC/NC、备注、参考文件与 8 个溯源新列 写入对应行，冲突行红色高亮，最后经 validate.py 硬性自检。 在用户拿到已应答/已复核 JSON 并要求"回填 Excel/生成应答后标书"时触发。 ([ZTE-AICloud/Co-Sight](https://github.com/ZTE-AICloud/Co-Sight/tree/HEAD/Co-Sight%20Harness/cosight-bot/skills/cosightbot-soc-fill) · ★1.1k)

- **cosightbot-soc-respond**: 标书需求 JSON 的批量历史范式应答：对提取出的需求列表逐条检索本地知识库的 历史应答范式，自动继承合规度（FC/PC/NC）、备注与参考文件，快速生成批量初步应答。 在用户要求"批量应答/对需求 JSON 做初步应答/跑一遍历史范式匹配"时触发。 输入为需求 JSON（cosightbot-soc-extract 的产物），输出 `&lt;stem&gt;-已应答.json`。 ([ZTE-AICloud/Co-Sight](https://github.com/ZTE-AICloud/Co-Sight/tree/HEAD/Co-Sight%20Harness/cosight-bot/skills/cosightbot-soc-respond) · ★1.1k)
- **cosightbot-soc-review**: 标书已应答 JSON 的大模型并发复核：逐条比对客户需求与知识库匹配文本， 死磕品牌、数值、版本号等关键冲突，判为不符合的条目标记 conflict=true， 产出 `&lt;stem&gt;-已大模型复核.json`。在用户要求"复核应答结果/检查应答冲突/ LLM 复核已应答 JSON"时触发。任何纯向量匹配命中的条目，交付前必须经本技能复核。 ([ZTE-AICloud/Co-Sight](https://github.com/ZTE-AICloud/Co-Sight/tree/HEAD/Co-Sight%20Harness/cosight-bot/skills/cosightbot-soc-review) · ★1.1k)

([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cosine-similarity-computation-msms))
- **cosine-similarity-confidence-interval-estimation**: Use when comparing spectral prediction checkpoints on a validation split and you need median cosine similarity plus uncertainty (95% CI) to select the configuration rather than trusting point estimates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cosine-similarity-confidence-interval-estimation))
- **cosine-similarity-decomposition-evaluation**: Use when a spot-level aggregated expression profile has been decomposed into per-cell expressions (e.g., via annealed Langevin posterior sampling) and you must quantify reconstruction accuracy against simulated ground truth. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cosine-similarity-decomposition-evaluation))
- **cosine-similarity-diff-for-fp8-numerics**: Compare two tensors with `1 - 2*sum(x*y)/sum(x^2 + y^2)` instead of absolute max-diff — scale-insensitive, handles all-zero tensors, and gives an interpretable "fraction of disagreement" for FP8/FP4 reference-testing. ([kjuhwa/skills-hub](https://github.com/kjuhwa/skills-hub/tree/HEAD/skills/testing-gpu/cosine-similarity-diff-for-fp8-numerics))
- **cosine-similarity-edge-thresholding**: Use when constructing a baseline molecular network from LC-MS/MS spectral pairs via cosine spectral similarity, or when reproducing/evaluating the Figure 1j,k experiment that shows which related metabolite pairs get fractured into separate clusters by a fixed cutoff. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cosine-similarity-edge-thresholding))
- **cosine-similarity-expression-reconstruction-assessment**: Use when you need to measure how much a GRN inference method (e.g., SCING vs ppcor, PIDC, GRNBOOST2) overfits, by comparing per-gene expression reconstruction quality (cosine similarity) on train vs held-out test cells from snRNA-seq or scRNA-seq data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cosine-similarity-expression-reconstruction-assessment))
- **cosine-similarity-fragment-matching**: Use when MS-DIAL-exported per-sample feature lists (precursor MS1 m/z, RT, MS/MS spectra) must be aligned/merged across samples into one non-redundant feature table for downstream classification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cosine-similarity-fragment-matching))
- **cosine-similarity-hash-collision-probability-analysis**: Use when you need to predict or tune the false-positive/false-negative trade-off of a random-projection LSH scheme — e.g., choosing (m,n) settings like (32,16), (64,32) or (32,32) before running signal classification on windowed MS data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cosine-similarity-hash-collision-probability-analysis))
- **cosine-similarity-kmer-abundance-scoring**: Use when you need all-vs-all distance matrices between FASTA/FASTQ metagenomic samples that retain both genetic-distance and abundance signal, e.g. for clustering 16S rRNA or WGS datasets at high resolution. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/cosine-similarity-kmer-abundance-scoring))
- **cosine-similarity-library-matching**: Use when query EI mass spectra must be identified against a spectral library that has coverage gaps, and candidate molecular structures (SMILES/SDF) are available so predicted spectra can fill those gaps. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cosine-similarity-library-matching))
- **cosine-similarity-matrix-computation**: Use when after generating normalized dense embeddings for both query and reference MS/MS spectra using a pre-trained model like SpecEmbedding. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/cosine-similarity-matrix-computation) · 2 copies)
- **cosine-similarity-metagenome-distance-computation**: Use when an all-vs-all distance matrix must be computed across many metagenome samples from full k-mer abundance vectors (no sketching or presence/absence reduction), and scale demands a single-pass, MapReduce-based sweep over an inverted k-mer index. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/cosine-similarity-metagenome-distance-computation))
- **cosine-similarity-metric-application**: Use when you are performing dimensionality reduction on a sparse single-cell count matrix (in CSR format) and need to compute pairwise cell similarities before spectral decomposition. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v1/skills/cosine-similarity-metric-application))
- **cosine-similarity-metric-interpretation**: Use when comparing a predicted MS/MS spectrum against a measured spectrum (e.g. retrieval-based MS2C ranking with Top-k) and needing to judge whether a cosine score reflects genuine spectral agreement rather than artifacts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cosine-similarity-metric-interpretation))
- **cosine-similarity-network-comparison**: Use when multiple sample-specific networks (e.g. pyPARAGON subnetworks per tumor) must be compared and grouped, via functional-module annotation profiles such as over-represented biological processes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/cosine-similarity-network-comparison))
- **cosine-similarity-one-sample-testing**: Use when a batch-corrected multi-subtype expression or proteomics matrix (e.g. FP/FS/NL vascular specimens) must be screened for subtype-specific marker proteins/genes and existing test statistics do not satisfy the MG definition. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cosine-similarity-one-sample-testing))
- **cosine-similarity-peak-vector-comparison**: Use when you have an experimental MS2 peak list and need per-compound spectral similarity scores against database reference spectra (e.g., MassBank) for metabolite annotation via CMM 3.0. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cosine-similarity-peak-vector-comparison))
- **cosine-similarity-range-validation**: Use when cot.df_cos has been produced by COT's generate_cos_values() (or the XOR-basis SDG variant) and the cosine values must be checked against theoretical bounds before ranking markers or writing sdg_cos_values.csv. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cosine-similarity-range-validation))
- **cosine-similarity-ranking**: Use when a composed query embedding (reference image pseudo-token fused with a text prompt in CLIP's text embedding space) must be matched against a fixed image-embedding database to produce a ranked top-k list or to re-rank after prompt changes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-agentic-ai](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-agentic-ai/tree/HEAD/collections/agentic-ai/v1/leaves/cosine-similarity-ranking))
- **cosine-similarity-ranking-metrics**: Use when when you have pre-computed spectral embeddings (vectors) for both query spectra and a reference library, and you need to measure retrieval performance by ranking reference compounds by similarity to each query. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/cosine-similarity-ranking-metrics) · 2 copies)
- **cosine-similarity-scoring**: Use when MZmine-exported feature tables (.csv with MS2-filtered rows) and paired fragmentation spectra (.mgf) need library annotation, and cosine scores plus matched-peak counts must be extracted per strain or sample node. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cosine-similarity-scoring--da5edb7c4fc162f8))
- **cosine-similarity-scoring**: Use when a mirror plot comparison of two tandem MS spectra is needed and peaks that differ by the precursor mass difference between the two spectra must also be matched, as the Spectrum Resolver does for metabolomics USI pairs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cosine-similarity-scoring--17becfbbd4f90fab))
- **cosine-similarity-scoring-computation**: Use when when you have imported and filtered mass spectrometry spectral data (from mzML, mzXML, msp, MGF, or JSON formats) and need to identify similar or related spectra within a dataset or against a reference library. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/cosine-similarity-scoring-computation) · 2 copies)
- **cosine-similarity-signature-matching**: Use when de novo NMF-extracted mutational signatures (SBS, DBS, or ID count-matrix based) need to be assigned COSMIC identity, or when validating that an extraction run reproduced reported signature counts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/cosine-similarity-signature-matching))
- **cosine-similarity-spectra-comparison**: Use when you need to quantify or remove near-duplicate spectra in an MGF-format MS/MS library (e.g., MassSpecGym or Spectraverse), by flagging same-compound spectra pairs with cosine similarity &gt;= 0.99. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cosine-similarity-spectra-comparison))
- **cosine-similarity-spectral-benchmark-scoring**: Use when comparing predicted spectra from C2MS models (FraGNNet, ICEBERG, GrAFF-MS, MassFormer, NEIMS, Precursor-Only) against real [M+H]+ Orbitrap HCD MS/MS test spectra to fill a Table 1-style prediction-error benchmark. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cosine-similarity-spectral-benchmark-scoring))
- **cosine-similarity-spectral-clustering**: Use when you have a collection of deconvolved mass spectra (in MGF or mzTab format) from GC-MS analysis and need to group them into a molecular network to identify structural relationships and enable compound annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/cosine-similarity-spectral-clustering) · 4 copies)
- **cosine-similarity-spectral-comparison**: Use when matching an observed EI mass spectrum against a library of candidate spectra and you need ranked similarity scores (e.g. recall@10 evaluation) under a molecular-mass filter. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cosine-similarity-spectral-comparison--dd8b87696302002d))
- **cosine-similarity-spectral-comparison**: Use when assigning or validating molecular formulas from untargeted LC/HRMS MS1 peaks: you have a candidate formula and need to rank or score it by how well its theoretical isotopic envelope matches the observed MS1 isotopic profile. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cosine-similarity-spectral-comparison--b6e9b64381ac417c))
- **cosine-similarity-spectral-matching**: Use when evaluating a structure-annotation confidence score (e.g. COSMIC) on clean reference spectra whose biological use case involves noisier spectra, or when quantifying spectral distortion/merging spectra per precursor feature by cosine distance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cosine-similarity-spectral-matching--56f93e88d50ba76c))
- **cosine-similarity-spectral-matching**: Use when you have centroided MS/MS data plus a feature table and want to cluster structurally related differential features (e.g. lipid classes like N-acyl GPEs, GLEAs, NAEs) into molecular subnetworks. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cosine-similarity-spectral-matching--af005bb773741195))
- **cosine-similarity-spectral-matching**: Use when dereplicating untargeted LC-MS2 .mzML features against public MS2 spectral libraries (GNPS, HMDB, MassBank) and you need library-level metabolite annotations with per-match similarity scores. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cosine-similarity-spectral-matching--a99b16179c240da9))
- **cosine-similarity-spectral-scoring**: Use when experimental MGF-format MS/MS spectra must be annotated by reference-library search against libraries such as Spectraverse, MassSpecGym, or NPLIB1, or when pairwise spectral similarity matrices are needed to evaluate preprocessing variants (centroiding, denoising, top-n ion retention). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cosine-similarity-spectral-scoring))
- **cosine-similarity-spectrum-loss**: Use when training or benchmarking MS/MS spectrum-prediction models (e.g., ICEBERG, SCARF, MassFormer, NEIMS) whose spectra must be compared to ground truth via binned-vector cosine similarity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cosine-similarity-spectrum-loss))
- **cosine-similarity-spectrum-matching**: Use when experimental MS/MS spectra (e.g. 29.1M spectra from human blood analyses on MetaboLights) must be annotated by spectral matching against a predicted in silico library, and you need to quantify how much library expansion (HMDB vs HMDB + DeepMet structures) increases matched spectra at a. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cosine-similarity-spectrum-matching))
- **cosine-spectral-angle-similarity-scoring**: Use when comparing two tandem MS datasets (MGF peak lists, e.g. from ProteomeXchange PXD034932) via spectrum-level cosine scores aggregated into compareMS2's symmetric set-level distance D(A,B) of eqs 1-6. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cosine-spectral-angle-similarity-scoring))
- **cosine-spectral-similarity-benchmarking**: Use when tandem-MS fragmentation spectra (fragment m/z lists plus precursor m/z values) are available and the goal is to test whether a new alignment method finds structural associations that cosine-based scoring misses, especially for compounds with multiple structural modifications. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cosine-spectral-similarity-benchmarking))
- **cosine-vs-euclidean-distance-evaluation**: Use when tuning a k-mer-feature-based sequence similarity search and you must decide which distance metric (cosine vs Euclidean) and feature weighting (TF, IDF, TF-IDF, raw) to feed an approximate nearest neighbor search such as NNDescent. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/cosine-vs-euclidean-distance-evaluation))

- **cosmo-galaxy-clustering**: Use when reviewing, planning or fact-checking spectroscopic galaxy or quasar clustering — BAO (D_M/r_d, D_H/r_d, D_V/r_d, reconstruction), RSD and fσ8, Alcock–Paczynski, full-shape, EFTofLSS or ShapeFit fits, f_NL from scale-dependent bias, Landy–Szalay or FKP/Yamamoto estimators, window functions, covariance, blinding, fibre collisions, PIP weights, imaging systematics, HOD — for 2dFGRS, 6dFGS, WiggleZ, BOSS, eBOSS, DESI DR1/DR2, Euclid, PFS, 4MOST, Roman, SPHEREx. ([corentinravoux/cosmoskill](https://github.com/corentinravoux/cosmoskill/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/cosmo-galaxy-halo-connection) · ★1)

- **cosmo-inflation-primordial**: Use when reviewing, planning or fact-checking inflation and primordial-physics claims — spectral index n_s and running, the ACT DR6 n_s shift, Starobinsky or α-attractor viability, tensor-to-scalar ratio r, B modes (BICEP/Keck BK18, SPT-3G, Simons Observatory, LiteBIRD), delensing, primordial non-Gaussianity f_NL (Planck bispectrum, scale-dependent bias, b_φ, DESI, SPHEREx, Euclid), isocurvature, spatial curvature Ω_K, primordial features, cosmic birefringence. - **cosmo-lyman-alpha**: Use when reviewing, planning or fact-checking Lyman-alpha forest work — Lyα BAO and Alcock-Paczynski at high redshift, full shape, 1D flux power spectrum P1D (FFT, QMLE), P3D and P-cross, picca deltas, continuum fitting, distortion matrix, DLA/BAL/metal contamination, Vega fits, Lyα tomography, IGM thermal state, Nyx/PRIYA/LaCE/ForestFlow emulators, warm or fuzzy dark matter and neutrino limits from the forest, BOSS/eBOSS/DESI DR1/DR2 Lyα results. - **cosmo-machine-learning**: Use when reviewing, planning or fact-checking machine learning in cosmology — neural emulators (CosmoPower, DarkEmulator2), CNN or GNN summaries, IMNN, VMIM, neural posterior, likelihood or ratio estimation, normalising flows, diffusion or flow-matching generative models, NECOLA, baryon painting, CAMELS, Quijote, CosmoGrid, PLAsTiCC, ELAsTiCC, FAIR Universe, SuperNNova, SCONE, anomaly detection, symbolic regression, robustness across simulators, misspecification. - **cosmo-multimessenger**: Use when reviewing, planning or fact-checking multi-messenger cosmology — standard sirens (bright, dark, spectral), GW170817 H0, peculiar velocity or inclination of a siren host, galaxy-catalogue completeness (GLADE+), LVK GWTC-3/4/5 H0, modified GW propagation, siren–galaxy cross-correlation, Einstein Telescope or LISA siren forecasts, fast radio bursts (dispersion measure–redshift relation, baryon fraction, H0), lensed supernovae (Refsdal, H0pe, Encore). - **cosmo-reionization-21cm**: Use when reviewing, planning or fact-checking reionisation or 21 cm work — reionisation history, optical depth tau, Gunn-Peterson troughs, dark pixels, Lyα damping wings, Lyα emitter fractions, patchy kSZ, neutral fraction x_HI, EDGES and SARAS global signal, 21 cm power-spectrum limits (HERA, LOFAR, MWA, NenuFAR), foreground wedge, HI intensity mapping (CHIME, MeerKAT, Tianlai, HIRAX, CHORD, SKA), line-intensity mapping (CO, [CII], COMAP, SPHEREx), JWST early galaxies. Use for C patches, hot loops, OpenMP/SIMD, GSL, FFTW/FAST-PT, nondeterministic chi2, perf benchmarks and performance claims. Applies to Limber and non-Limber calculations, TATT, NLA, 3x2pt, Legendre sums, tomographic C_ell, halo models and HOD, even without optimization work. - **cosmological-boltzmann-solver-benchmarking**: Use when measuring wall-clock performance or thread scaling of a cosmological Boltzmann solver run (e.g. SymBoltz integrating many k-modes), or when validating that an approximation-free implementation achieves performance comparable to approximation-based codes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy/tree/HEAD/collections/astronomy/v1/leaves/cosmological-boltzmann-solver-benchmarking))
- **cosmological-catalogue-pair-counting**: Use when measuring two-point or three-point correlation functions from real or simulated extragalactic catalogues, where naive O(N^2) pair/triplet enumeration over all objects is prohibitively slow. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy/tree/HEAD/collections/astronomy/v1/leaves/cosmological-catalogue-pair-counting))
- **cosmological-growth-rate-evaluation**: Use when the goal is a numerical value of the linear growth rate fσ8 at a specific redshift under a specified cosmology, and an external Boltzmann solver (CAMB) may be used as the computation backend. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy/tree/HEAD/collections/astronomy/v1/leaves/cosmological-growth-rate-evaluation))
- **cosmological-n-body-simulation-execution**: Reference for running particle-based large-scale-structure simulations (e.g. N=1024^3 particles in 2048/1024/512/256 Mpc/h boxes) when output must be validated against GADGET-2 to sub-per-mille/1% accuracy. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy/tree/HEAD/collections/astronomy/v1/leaves/cosmological-n-body-simulation-execution))
- **cosmological-observation**: Observational cosmology from Hubble's law to the CMB. Covers redshift, Hubble expansion, the cosmological parameters, the cosmic microwave background, large-scale structure, galaxy rotation curves and dark matter, Type Ia SNe and dark energy, and the current state of Lambda-CDM. Use when reasoning about the large-scale universe, interpreting cosmological surveys, or teaching the Big Bang evidence chain. ([Tibsfox/gsd-skill-creator](https://github.com/Tibsfox/gsd-skill-creator/tree/HEAD/examples/skills/astronomy/cosmological-observation) · ★71)
- **cosmological-parameter-configuration**: Use when a CBL modelling task requires a non-default cosmology: the example constructors apply Planck-like defaults, but mass function, bias, growth-rate (fσ8) or 2PCF computations need explicitly set parameters. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy/tree/HEAD/collections/astronomy/v1/leaves/cosmological-parameter-configuration--1c47a2425a753bf1))
- **cosmological-parameter-configuration**: Use when a cosmological analysis requires setting up a Boltzmann-solver model with explicit cosmological parameters (H0, Ωm, Ωb, w0, wa, c_s^2, lmax, reionization) that must be reproduced identically across two codes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy/tree/HEAD/collections/astronomy/v1/leaves/cosmological-parameter-configuration--af960eb12c6805e7))
- **cosmological-parameter-configuration-for-hmf**: Use when setting up or varying the cosmology (Omega_c, Omega_Lambda, sigma_8, spectral index, transfer function source, growth factor) underlying an hmf/HMFcalc halo mass function calculation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy/tree/HEAD/collections/astronomy/v1/leaves/cosmological-parameter-configuration-for-hmf))
- **cosmological-parameter-error-propagation**: Use when a Fisher-forecast-style error budget is needed for CMB or lensing power-spectrum data (e.g. SPT-3G TT/TE/EE, ACT phi-phi) at a fixed fiducial cosmology, and exact derivatives are preferred over finite differences. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy/tree/HEAD/collections/astronomy/v1/leaves/cosmological-parameter-error-propagation))
- **cosmological-parameter-grid-sampling**: Use when benchmarking speed or accuracy of cosmological emulators (e.g. symbolic_pofk plin_emulated() vs CAMB vs BACCO) — identical cosmology/k grids are required for fair per-point ratio computation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy/tree/HEAD/collections/astronomy/v1/leaves/cosmological-parameter-grid-sampling))
- **cosmological-parameter-interpretation**: Use when a learned symbolic expression for the linear matter power spectrum (or similar emulator formula) needs each subterm annotated with its physical role and cosmological-parameter dependence, e.g. to show interpretability beyond black-box fitting. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy/tree/HEAD/collections/astronomy/v1/leaves/cosmological-parameter-interpretation))
- **cosmological-parameter-log-derivative-analysis**: Use when exact derivatives of power spectra or CMB spectra with respect to cosmological parameters are needed for Fisher forecasting, gradient-based MCMC, emulator training, or sensitivity analyses, and when AD Jacobians must be validated against an established Boltzmann solver. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy/tree/HEAD/collections/astronomy/v1/leaves/cosmological-parameter-log-derivative-analysis))
- **cosmological-parameter-mcmc-sampling**: Use when a task requires setting up or benchmarking MCMC posterior sampling of cosmological parameters (H0, Omega_b, Omega_cdm, tau, n_s, logA, sigma8) from CMB likelihoods, or when reproducing autocorrelation times, burn-in lengths, or epsilon-convergence comparisons between ensemble and. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy/tree/HEAD/collections/astronomy/v1/leaves/cosmological-parameter-mcmc-sampling))
- **cosmological-parameter-sampler-setup**: Use when you need to sample a posterior over cosmological parameters (e.g. 6 WMAP-constrained CMB parameters) with emcee/CosmoHammer, requiring explicit parameter bounds, walker counts, burn-in/sample iterations, and chain module wiring. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy/tree/HEAD/collections/astronomy/v1/leaves/cosmological-parameter-sampler-setup))
- **cosmological-parameter-sensitivity-benchmarking**: Use when you need exact derivatives of cosmological observables (power spectra P(k), CMB D_l) with respect to parameters like h, Ωc0, Ωb0, YHe for Fisher forecasting, gradient-based MCMC, or AD runtime benchmarking. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy/tree/HEAD/collections/astronomy/v1/leaves/cosmological-parameter-sensitivity-benchmarking))
- **cosmological-simulation-merger-tree-analysis**: Use when you have halo merger tree catalogs (e.g., from TNG or gravity-only simulations) and need to model, fit, or evaluate individual halo mass accretion histories log(Mhalo) vs. cosmic time. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy/tree/HEAD/collections/astronomy/v1/leaves/cosmological-simulation-merger-tree-analysis))
- **cosmological-simulation-parameter-reproduction**: Reference for reproducing a published cosmological simulation (e.g. the 512 Mpc/h, N=1024^3 CONCEPT 1.0 vs GADGET-4 comparison) when the goal is to recreate runs from released parameter files and validate code outputs against reported agreement levels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy/tree/HEAD/collections/astronomy/v1/leaves/cosmological-simulation-parameter-reproduction))
- **cosmological-snapshot-particle-classification**: Use when a simulation snapshot (positions, velocities, masses) needs per-particle bound/unbound labels from a topology-based binding criterion that relies only on instantaneous information and no ad hoc thresholds. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy/tree/HEAD/collections/astronomy/v1/leaves/cosmological-snapshot-particle-classification))
- **cosmological-software-api-usage**: Use when a task requires chaining cosmological calculation stages (e.g. parameter modules that read/write a shared state object) in a fixed order using the CosmoSIS Python API and its DataBlock data flow. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy/tree/HEAD/collections/astronomy/v1/leaves/cosmological-software-api-usage))
- **cosmological-table-export**: Use when you need to reproduce and save a tabulated dark matter halo mass function (M vs dn/dlnM) from hmf, e.g. to share HMF values for a given cosmology, fitting function and mass range as a portable CSV file. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy/tree/HEAD/collections/astronomy/v1/leaves/cosmological-table-export))
- **cosmological-time-step-limiter-analysis**: Use when analysing or reproducing how the allowed global time step Δln a in a cosmological N-body code (specifically CONCEPT 1.0) evolves across cosmic history and which limiter governs at each scale factor and resolution. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy/tree/HEAD/collections/astronomy/v1/leaves/cosmological-time-step-limiter-analysis))
- **cosmological-wavenumber-grid-handling**: Reference for ingesting a CAMB-format transfer function T(k), interpolating it in log-space with linear splines, and re-gridding it onto hmf's k-bounds before power-spectrum or HMF computation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy/tree/HEAD/collections/astronomy/v1/leaves/cosmological-wavenumber-grid-handling))
- **cosmologist**: Think and work like an expert Cosmologist. Use when a task calls for Cosmologist judgment. Reasons from Friedmann/ΛCDM, r_s and transfer functions, and multi-probe inference (Planck CMB, DESI BAO, lensing, Pantheon+ SNe) through CAMB/CLASS, Cobaya, and GetDist while treating photo-z–IA coupling, CMB foreground pipelines, H0/S8 tensions, and emulator extrapolation as first-class failure modes. ([K-Dense-AI/scientific-agents](https://github.com/K-Dense-AI/scientific-agents/tree/HEAD/scientific-agents/cosmologist/skills/cosmologist) · ★255 · 2 copies)
